- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-modeling**: Use when you are generating synthetic LC-MS/MS data for method validation, algorithm benchmarking, or co-fragmentation analysis, and need to model how specific biomolecules (peptides, nucleosides, or other chemical formulas) fragment under collision-induced dissociation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-modeling) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-motif-visualization**: Use when a trained topic model over MS/MS fragmentation data (Mass2Motifs assigned to spectra) needs interactive visual exploration before motif characterization, e.g. filtering motifs by degree prior to biochemical interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-motif-visualization))
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-pattern-discovery**: Use when you have preprocessed MS/MS spectral data (in positive or negative ion mode) and seek to discover recurring fragmentation and neutral-loss patterns that characterize molecular substructures across a dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-pattern-discovery) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-pattern-modeling**: Use when when building a re-usable spectral reference library for lipidomics workflows where you need to match experimental MS/MS spectra against a comprehensive theoretical fragmentation model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-pattern-modeling) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-scoring**: Use when evaluating small-molecule identification accuracy of a mass spectral database-search method on spectra with known true structures, such as benchmarking top-1/3/5/10 accuracy on the PNNL lipid library against LMSD compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-scoring))
- **mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-spectrum-processing**: Use when you have paired genome + LC-MS/MS samples and need to turn raw fragmentation spectra (GNPS job output or cryptic spectra) into per-sample MS/MS fingerprints for BGC linking or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-spectrum-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-identification**: Identify and quantify support for analytes from mass spectrometry with explicit acquisition context, calibration, false-discovery control, and isomer-aware evidence. Use for GC-MS, LC-MS, MS/MS, accurate mass, isotope patterns, adducts, fragments, library searching, proteomics, metabolomics, and unknown identification. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/mass-spectrometry-identification) · ★4)
- **mass-spectrometry-image-linescan-projection**: Use when you have 2D DESI-MSI hyperspectral images of a tissue with an approximate linear developmental gradient (e.g., maize root tip) and need to assign mass signatures to developmental enrichment clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-image-linescan-projection))
- **mass-spectrometry-image-normalization**: Use when after loading a pixel array (NumPy format) and its associated metadata JSON file from MSIGen, when you need to account for pixel-to-pixel variations in total ion signal or when comparing relative abundances of multiple ions within or across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-image-normalization) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-image-preprocessing**: Use when when preparing raw mass spectrometry imaging (MSI) ion images for deep learning–based representation learning, especially when you need to generate augmented image pairs that preserve domain-specific artifacts (photon counting noise, missing pixels) while varying appearance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/mass-spectrometry-image-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectrometry-image-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw or preprocessed single-channel (2D array) or multi-channel (spectral) ion images from mass spectrometry imaging (MSI) data and need to train a contrastive deep learning model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-image-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectrometry-image-reconstruction**: Use when you have paired .imzML (XML metadata) and . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-image-reconstruction))
- **mass-spectrometry-image-segmentation**: Use when a peak-picked MSI dataset (e.g., imzML loaded into Cardinal as a processed MSImagingExperiment) must be partitioned into chemically homogeneous tissue segments without class labels, and the expected number of segments is known from a reference figure or must be selected by scanning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-image-segmentation))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-classification**: Use when raw MALDI MSI spectra (e.g. imzML/HDF5 with tens of thousands of m/z variables) must be classified into tissue classes (normal vs tumor) with per-pixel probabilities, and the probabilistic outputs must be mapped back into image space to localize classification uncertainty such as at tumor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-classification))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-clustering**: Use when imzML MSI pixel spectra need unsupervised segmentation into tissue clusters, especially when t-SNE embedding parameters (perplexity, learning rate, iterations) must be tuned and compared via Silhouette score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-clustering))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-import**: Use when you have raw MS imaging data in imzML (continuous or processed) or Analyze 7.5 format and need to load it into R for spectral processing, normalization, peak-picking, or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-import))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-interpretation**: Use when when you have preprocessed MALDI-MSI data (in msimat format) and want to determine whether abundant peaks are actually molecular adducts of simpler parent ions rather than distinct metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-loading**: Use when you have raw or preprocessed MS imaging data archived as an RDS file or from a Zenodo deposit that includes the full m/z feature set (e.g., 10,200 m/z values spanning 150–1000 m/z range) and spectrum count (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-loading) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-processing**: Use when you have raw or processed MSI data (in imzML or rMSIproc formats) and need to identify and annotate matrix-related peaks before statistical analysis or metabolite identification. Use it specifically when your MSI experiment employed a chemical matrix (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-processing**: Use when you have DESI-MSI imzML/NetCDF (cdf) pixel spectra from elongating root sections arranged along a linear developmental gradient and need to identify metabolite mass signatures and group them by developmental enrichment pattern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-representation**: Use when you have raw mass spectrometry imaging data (2D or 3D spatial coordinates with full mass-to-charge spectra) and want to train a probabilistic deep learning classifier for tumor delineation or tissue classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-data-representation))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when pixel-wise peak features from one or multiple MALDI MSI samples must be jointly projected into a low-dimensional embedding (UMAP) and density-clustered (HDBSCAN) for segmentation or sample-outlier flagging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-dimensionality-reduction))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-line-scan-data-handling**: Use when you have raw line-scan MSI data from a vendor instrument (Agilent, Bruker, Thermo, or open-source .mzML format) and need to extract ion images for specified m/z targets with spatial binning and tolerance-based filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-line-scan-data-handling) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-peak-table-processing**: Use when a spatial metabolomics peak table matrix (pixels x m/z ion peaks with X/Y coordinates) contains background pixels and randomly distributed noise ions that must be detected and filtered before spatial pattern recognition or clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-peak-table-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-preprocessing**: Use when raw DESI-MSI root-imaging data (NetCDF .cdf or MATLAB workspace .mat files) must be converted into per-sample mass signature lists before gradient clustering or cross-replicate comparison. - **mass-spectrometry-imaging-signal-preprocessing**: Use when raw MALDI-MSI imzML pixel spectra must be made comparable across a tissue section (TIC normalization), denoised, and background-corrected before clustering or segmentation with MSIght. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-signal-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-spectra-embedding**: Use when a trained massNet-style VAE over high-dimensional MALDI MSI spectra (e.g., 85,062 m/z variables) exists and you need to inspect its 5-D latent space or check normal-vs-tumor separability in a 2D UMAP map. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-imaging-spectra-embedding))
- **mass-spectrometry-imaging-vendor-format-support**: Use when your input mass spectrometry imaging data is in a proprietary vendor format (Bruker .d/.baf, or other binary formats not natively supported by MSIGen) and you need to convert it to an open, readable format (mzML or processed binary) compatible with MSIGen's msigen() function. - **mass-spectrometry-instrument-format-compatibility**: Use when you have raw mass spectrometry data from an instrument not yet validated in your pipeline (e. - **mass-spectrometry-instrument-resolution-modeling**: Use when when correcting LC-MS fractional abundances of measured isotopologues (FAM) from isotope labeling experiments where instrument resolution (e. - **mass-spectrometry-intensity-drift-correction**: Use when mS quantification data exhibits intensity drift—a systematic decline or variation in detector response across the run sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-intensity-drift-correction))
- **mass-spectrometry-intensity-encoding**: Use when converting vendor MRM/MS raw files to a compact, computation-oriented binary format and file size or fast web-side rendering of hundreds of chromatograms is the goal. - **mass-spectrometry-intensity-filtering**: Use when MS/MS spectra contain many low-intensity noise peaks — e.g. prior to peak annotation, library comparison, or spectrum visualization — and you need to restrict peaks to a fraction of the base peak intensity and cap their number. - **mass-spectrometry-ion-formula-assignment**: Use when when you have a metabolite structure (SMILES or molecular graph) and need to predict its ionization behavior in a mass spectrometry experiment using a specific derivatizing matrix or ionization mode. - **mass-spectrometry-ionization-mechanism-interpretation**: Decision support for choosing an ionization source (EI 70 eV, EI 15 eV, CI-methane, CI-isobutane) when designing or interpreting GC-MS isotopologue detection for 13C/15N labeling experiments. - **mass-spectrometry-ionization-mode-handling**: Use when when you have a feature table from LC-MS preprocessed data (e.g. from asari v1.9.2) and need to annotate ions and infer neutral mass. - **mass-spectrometry-library-curation**: Use when when preprocessing a public MS/MS spectral library (e.g., GNPS) for machine learning and you discover discrepancies between expected and observed compound counts after filtering by instrument type, or when a known instrument metadata issue (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-library-curation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-library-downloading**: Use when you need to quantify the adduct/ion-species composition of public LC-MS2 spectral libraries (MoNA, GNPS) or need those raw library entries as inputs for an ion-species bias analysis. - **mass-spectrometry-library-indexing**: Use when you have a large MS/MS experiment (mzML format) requiring lipid annotation and need to match experimental spectra against &gt;10 million theoretical lipid fragments. - **mass-spectrometry-library-matching**: Use when when you have an unknown MS/MS spectrum (with ≥10 peaks, precursor m/z, and at least 5 fragment ions) and need to identify it by comparing against a curated spectral library with annotated InChIKeys or chemical structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-library-matching) · 4 copies)

- **mass-spectrometry-library-search-retrieval**: Use when when you have an unknown MSMS spectrum (precursor m/z and fragment ions) and need to discover structurally related compounds from a spectral library. - **mass-spectrometry-m-z-accuracy-assessment**: Use when when you have detected peaks in a direct injection FTICR-MS mzML file (or similar high-resolution MS format) and need to assess whether m/z measurements are accurate and consistent across the m/z range. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-m-z-accuracy-assessment) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-mass-accuracy-and-precision-tuning**: Use when when extracting and validating chromatographic peaks for target molecules from centroided mzML files, particularly when working with multiple isotopologues and adducts where mass tolerance directly affects whether predicted isotopologues are correctly grouped and detected as part of the. - **mass-spectrometry-mass-peak-matching**: Use when you have a target molecular weight (1–5 decimal digits) from a high-resolution mass spectrum of an unknown natural-product mixture and need to rank candidate structures from seed scaffolds (.cIdx files). - **mass-spectrometry-match-factor-filtering**: Use when you have a GC-MS dataset with a Match.Factor column (output from Agilent Unknowns Analysis or equivalent) and need to retain only high-confidence compound identifications. - **mass-spectrometry-match-table-compilation**: Use when you have MASST search results (per-compound job/USI identifiers) for a panel of reference tandem MS spectra and must aggregate them into a per-compound match table with deduplicated file counts. - **mass-spectrometry-match-thresholding**: Use when a MASST or GNPS search result table of candidate spectral matches for a synthesized compound's MS/MS spectrum must be filtered to trusted matches before phenotypic association analysis. - **mass-spectrometry-matrix-background-simulation**: Use when when you need to create negative control or background-only reference datasets for LC/GC-MS analysis pipelines—specifically to validate peak-picking algorithms, assess false-positive rates, or simulate instrument background and matrix effects (e. - **mass-spectrometry-metadata-caching**: Use when you plan to perform repeated queries or filtering on MS metadata attributes (e.g., extract all MS2 spectra with collision energy &gt; 30 eV, or collect all scans in a retention time window) across a large MZA HDF5 file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-metadata-caching) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-metadata-extraction**: Use when Thermo RAW files (e.g. downloaded from PRIDE accessions) need instrument-, run-, and scan-level metadata extracted into structured TSV/CSV tables for QC, repository submission, or batch annotation workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-metadata-extraction--659f485ade8a458d))
- **mass-spectrometry-metadata-extraction**: Use when you have peak/feature tables from one or more of MZmine, XCMS, MS-DIAL, or Compound Discoverer and need to integrate them into a unified lipidomics workflow (e.g., LipidMatch). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-metadata-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-metadata-extraction**: Use when input contains mzspec:/mzdraft: USI strings that must be split into collection, msRun, indexType, indexNumber fields and mapped to the correct metabolomics data repository or spectral library service. - **mass-spectrometry-metadata-integration**: Use when a GNPS molecular networking job must be tied back to sample context: uploading a standardized metadata table with the LC-MS/MS files and classifying network nodes by sample origin (e.g., lung tissue vs. cultured isolates). - **mass-spectrometry-metadata-interpretation**: Use when when integrating LC-MS/MS data from diverse sources (e.g., public repositories like MSV000080102, instrument outputs, or precomputed workflows) into NPDtools pipelines. - **mass-spectrometry-metadata-parsing**: Use when you have a Thermo Fisher Orbitrap .raw file and need to programmatically inspect or validate its acquisition parameters (instrument type, total scan count, acquisition duration, file name) before extracting spectral data, or when you need to serialize metadata to JSON for data provenance. - **mass-spectrometry-metadata-validation**: Use when after importing raw LC-MS/MS data files into the SIRIUS Java framework, before constructing indexed spectrum objects or submitting data to CSI:FingerID, CANOPUS, or MSNovelist web services. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-metadata-validation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-method-cycle-verification**: Use when when you have a Thermo Fisher Scientific Orbitrap .raw file and need to confirm that a targeted acquisition method (e.g., PRM targeting a specific precursor m/z) is achieving uniform cycle timing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-method-cycle-verification) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-metric-engineering**: Use when you have raw DIA mass spectrometry files (.raw, .d, .wiff formats) from timsTOF, TripleTOF, or Orbitrap instruments and need to quantify file quality for automated quality control, longitudinal instrument monitoring, or training a quality prediction classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-metric-engineering) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-molecular-formula-handling**: Use when you have raw or processed FT-ICR MS spectra with detected peaks (m/z values) and need to: (1) assign elemental compositions to each peak, (2) filter assignments by mass error tolerance and isotopic presence, or (3) prepare a peak table with molecular formula annotations for chemodiversity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/mass-spectrometry-molecular-formula-handling) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-molecular-networking**: Use when an untargeted tandem MS dataset has been processed with GNPS feature-based molecular networking and in silico annotations (e.g., CANOPUS summaries) are available, and consensus chemical classifications must be propagated across subnetworks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-molecular-networking))
- **mass-spectrometry-mz-binning**: Use when raw NetCDF/CDF files from comprehensive 2D GC–MS runs must be folded into a sample-by-m/z-by-RT2-by-RT1 array and the m/z axis must be rounded and aligned to the acquired fragment range before peak picking or deconvolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-mz-binning))
- **mass-spectrometry-mz-deviation-tracking**: Use when tracking the long-term QC pool on a Waters Synapt instrument and you need to compute per-injection m/z deviations (ppm) from target m/z values and flag deviations &gt; 20 ppm as serious analytical problems. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-mz-deviation-tracking))
- **mass-spectrometry-network-construction**: Use when you have deconvolved GC-MS spectra (output from auto-deconvolution) and want to group similar spectra into clusters to discover spectral families, identify co-eluting compounds, or organize unknown metabolites by structural similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-network-construction) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-noise-injection**: Use when generating a synthetic mzML with SMITER and the simulated run must carry a realistic baseline intensity noise, e.g. to compare simulated vs. real TIC noise profiles as in the 24-nucleoside-standard validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-noise-injection))
- **mass-spectrometry-novelty-prioritization**: Use when a feature-based molecular networking (FBMN) experiment produces multiannotated MS/MS features and you need to prioritize putatively novel natural products by ranking features with a knownness score instead of relying on single-tool annotation ranks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-novelty-prioritization))
- **mass-spectrometry-outlier-detection**: Use when you have multi-sample MS1 data (LC-MS, LC-IMS-MS, or direct infusion across any omics domain) and need to detect samples with abnormal global ion intensity patterns or unusual per-ion metric behavior (intensity distribution, signal-to-noise, retention time stability) that may indicate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-outlier-detection) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-package-integration**: Use when you have R Spectra objects and need to apply Python-only MS algorithms (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-package-integration) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-parameter-spreadsheet-configuration**: Use when you have IDSL.IPA-generated 'peaklists' and 'peak_alignment' directories from LC/HRMS MS1 data and need to run CSA correlation-based deconvolution by filling and submitting the IDSL.CSA parameter spreadsheet. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-parameter-spreadsheet-configuration))
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-alignment-visualization**: Use when when you have aligned peak data from molecular networking (with m/z, intensity, retention time, and alignment quality metrics across multiple spectra) and need to interactively explore peak alignments under multiple filtering criteria (intensity thresholds, alignment score cutoffs, peak. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-alignment-visualization) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-area-extraction**: Use when you have macroMS-exported per-target mass spectra (peak areas at known m/z values) and need per-sample quantitative features, such as fatty acyl ratios, for comparing groups of samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-area-extraction))
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-classification**: Use when you have raw mzML files and feature tables (CSV format from mzMine or XCMS) from untargeted LCMS experiments and need to distinguish true metabolite peaks from false positives introduced by the peak-picking algorithm. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-classification) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-data-loading**: Use when when you have raw or parsed MS spectra data (m/z values, intensity values, and spectral metadata) and need to integrate it into a Spectra object for downstream processing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-data-loading))
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-detection-and-alignment**: Use when after normalization, smoothing, and baseline reduction have been performed on raw MSImagingArrays data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-detection-and-alignment))
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-detection-and-extraction**: Use when you have raw high-resolution mass-spectrometry data in mzML or mzXML format from breath analysis and need to identify individual volatile organic compounds before sample-to-sample alignment or comparative analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-detection-and-extraction))
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-enumeration**: Use when you have preprocessed MSI data (peaks already binned and normalized) and need to detect adduct formation patterns across the dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-enumeration))
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-filtering-and-noise-reduction**: Use when you have raw MS/MS spectra in MGF, mzML, or msp format and need to prepare them for mass2motif discovery or topic modeling. - **mass-spectrometry-peak-identification-and-extraction**: Use when when you have raw mass spectrometry data from direct-infusion (DI-MS) or ambient surface analysis probe (ASAP-MS) instruments and need to identify which m/z peaks are biologically or chemically informative for sample classification, rather than processing the entire spectrum including. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-identification-and-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-intensity-encoding**: Use when working with imaging mass spectrometry (IMS) datasets where you need to (1) automatically identify marker ions without manual annotation, (2) reduce peak intensity dimensionality while preserving spatial relationships between measurement points, or (3) apply iterative peak picking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-intensity-encoding) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-list-processing**: Use when raw MS/MS spectra (mzML, mzXML, msp, MGF, JSON, or metabolomics-USI) contain inconsistent metadata, noisy peaks, or incompatible intensity scales and must be cleaned, filtered, and normalized before similarity scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-list-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-metrics-extraction**: Use when batch LC-MS, LC-IMS-MS, DDA/DIA, or direct-infusion raw MS data (Agilent 'd', Thermo '.raw', Bruker 'd', or mzML) needs precursor-ion QC metrics and error reports without omics-specific identification pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peak-metrics-extraction))
- **mass-spectrometry-peak-pair-analysis**: Use when you have preprocessed MSI data (as a CSV intensity matrix or Cardinal MSProcessedImagingExperiment object) and suspect that observed peaks include both parent ions and their adducts formed with matrix or salt species. - **mass-spectrometry-peak-validation**: Use when after peak alignment across all spectra in an MSImagingExperiment using peakAlign(), when you need to reduce the feature set to high-confidence peaks by removing spurious or low-frequency detections. - **mass-spectrometry-peptide-superset-validation**: Use when validating that peptides identified by mass spectrometry against a novel isoform-derived ORF catalog are absent from a standard reference proteome (e.g., UniProt mouse) and confirming a published count of unannotated peptides. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/mass-spectrometry-peptide-superset-validation))
- **mass-spectrometry-pipeline-component-mapping**: Use when you need to decompose an MS analysis framework (e.g. OpenMS) into its constituent layers and components — such as deciding whether to extend the C++ API, wrap a TOPP tool, or reuse a pre-built workflow. - **mass-spectrometry-plot-taxonomy-implementation**: Use when you are building a visualization library that must support multiple plot kinds (chromatogram, spectrum, mobilogram, peakmap) across multiple rendering backends (matplotlib, bokeh, plotly) and want to avoid code duplication. - **mass-spectrometry-plot-type-specialization**: Use when you have a Pandas DataFrame containing mass spectrometry data (e. - **mass-spectrometry-polarity-filtering**: Use when when processing centroided .mzML LC–MS runs with a multi-polarity target list (i.e., some targets ionize in positive mode, others in negative mode, or both) and you need to detect peaks and extract ion chromatograms without manually subsetting the raw data by polarity beforehand. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-polarity-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-precursor-identification**: Use when when you need to locate and extract quantitative retention time and intensity data for known peptide standards (e.g., iRT peptides) from a Thermo .raw file to validate LC-MS retention time linearity, assess method reproducibility, or establish retention time calibration curves. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-precursor-identification) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-prediction-modeling**: Use when when you have a collection of compound structures in SDF format (e. - **mass-spectrometry-preprocessing**: Use when raw MS/MS data from complex mixtures (e.g., beer or urine extracts on a Q-Exactive Orbitrap) must be converted into fragment/neutral-loss documents before unsupervised substructure discovery with LDA. - **mass-spectrometry-preprocessing**: Use when when you have raw mass-spectrometry data (precursor m/z, ionization mode, and fragment m/z–intensity pairs) that must be fed into a CNN model for metabolite annotation via compound fingerprint prediction. - **mass-spectrometry-preprocessing-quality-control**: Use when immediately after importing raw peak tables and metadata from MS preprocessing software (e.g., Progenesis, MS-DIAL, Bruker Metaboscape). - **mass-spectrometry-priority-rank-reproduction**: Use when a raw MZmine feature quantification table plus GNPS FBMN metadata must be converted into a ranked extract list with FC, FS, LC, CC and SC components to prioritize extracts for novel-compound isolation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-priority-rank-reproduction))
- **mass-spectrometry-processing-benchmark-timing**: Use when a task asks to verify or reproduce the reported processing-speed benchmark for rtmsEcho or similar AEMS pipelines, e.g. timing a full 384-well plate full-scan (PI) run against the &lt;30 s (up to 60 Da window) claim. - **mass-spectrometry-qc-criteria-definition**: Use when when setting up a new LC-MS QC workflow or modifying existing QC rules: you have access to internal standards and target analytes, know their expected retention times and m/z values, and need to establish pass/fail boundaries for sample acceptance before or concurrent with instrument runs. - **mass-spectrometry-qc-metrics**: Use when evaluating Thermo Fisher RAW-file acquisition quality — e.g. verifying that MS2 scan fractions (e.g. 60%/62% reference vs 56%/59% underperforming) or scan-level TIC/injection-time trends match expected instrument behaviour. - **mass-spectrometry-qc-plot-generation**: Use when peak metrics for auto-tracked MS1 ions or user-specified theoretical/reference ion targets have already been extracted and you need per-ion, per-metric CSV reports plus JPG/PDF figures to inspect and track instrument performance. - **mass-spectrometry-qc-tabulation**: Use when vendor-agnostic QC of LC-HRMS acquisitions is needed: computing per-analyte retention time RSD, peak areas, or extracted-ion intensities from SST mixture mzML files to assess instrument performance before data collection. - **mass-spectrometry-quality-control-image-generation**: Use when performing batch-level quality assessment of untargeted LC-MS, LC-IMS-MS, or direct-infusion MS1 data (Agilent 'd', Thermo '.raw', Bruker 'd', or mzML) and you need a per-sample visual/PCA representation before extracting detailed peak metrics. - **mass-spectrometry-quality-metrics-extraction**: Use when you have centroided .mzML LC–MS data, a validated target compound list with adjusted expected retention times (e.g., after a screening mode run), and need to quantify peak quality and integration reliability across multiple sample runs to support metabolomics or lipidomics workflows. - **mass-spectrometry-quantification**: Use when you have measured MS intensity data from unknown samples and known-concentration standard compounds, and you need to convert sample intensities into predicted concentrations. - **mass-spectrometry-quantification-extraction**: Use when you have raw LipidSearch or LIQUID output files (CSV or TSV format) containing lipid identifiers and per-sample quantification measurements, and you need to convert them into a machine-readable data matrix for downstream statistical analysis, normalization, or differential abundance. - **mass-spectrometry-query-semantics**: Use when when you need to express complex mass spectrometry search patterns (e.g., isotope envelope detection, neutral loss patterns, intensity relationships across m/z ranges) in a human-readable format that can be executed against spectral data files (mzML, mzXML) or spectral repositories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-reference-standard-mapping) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-representation-learning**: Use when raw MS vectors (e.g. TIC-normalized spectra with spectrum_dim 9900–15000) need a richer feature representation than a single-channel 1D vector, to improve classification accuracy and computational efficiency of CNN classifiers like ResNet50 or DenseNet121. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-representation-learning))
- **mass-spectrometry-resolution-aware-peak-shape-modeling**: Use when a target metabolite's molecular formula and adduct are known and you need resolution-aware theoretical isotope patterns or tracer-isotopologue m/z windows as target lists for downstream quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-resolution-aware-peak-shape-modeling))
- **mass-spectrometry-result-tabulation**: Use when after executing a MassQL query against mzML mass spectrometry data files and obtaining tabulated results (DataFrame or equivalent in-memory table), apply this skill to persist those results in both human-readable CSV format and visual image form for archival, sharing, and downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-result-tabulation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing runtime efficiency of mass spectrometry processing libraries on identical peak-processing steps (m/z trimming, precursor/noise removal, intensity filtering and scaling) over MGF spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-runtime-benchmarking))
- **mass-spectrometry-sample-traceability**: Use when preparing an injection worksheet for GC/LC-MS metabolomics/proteomics where QC samples must occupy a dedicated plate area, well positions must not interpenetrate experimental wells, and injection order must be traceable independently of well layout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-sample-traceability))
- **mass-spectrometry-scan-extraction-by-target**: Use when you have raw LC-MS/MS chromatogram files in mzML/mzXML format (converted from Thermo, Waters, or Bruker instruments) acquired in DDA or targeted MS/MS mode, and you need to isolate specific MS1 precursors and their corresponding MS2 fragments based on known m/z values and optional. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-scan-extraction-by-target) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-scan-indexing**: Use when you have a Thermo Fisher Scientific .raw file and need to (1) enumerate all scans and their metadata, (2) identify which scans are MS1 vs. MSn to enable level-specific filtering, (3) retrieve scan ranges or specific scan numbers for targeted spectral extraction, or (4) plan. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-scan-indexing) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-scoring-method-validation**: Use when you have high-resolution LC-MS data processed through both XCMS feature detection and RAMClustR clustering, and you need to verify the reliability of molecular weight assignments before downstream annotation or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-scoring-method-validation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-screening-workflows**: Use when when you have high-resolution LC-MS or GC-MS data from environmental samples and need to simultaneously screen for both known suspect chemicals and their transformation products, rather than targeting single compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-screening-workflows) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-seasonality-analysis**: Use when a metabolomics dataset (e.g. mzML/mzXML from MassIVE MSV000097575 wastewater influent, n=55) has time-stamped samples and you need to quantify temporal drug-exposure trends from untargeted MS/MS annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-seasonality-analysis))
- **mass-spectrometry-signal-classification-evaluation**: Use when labeled synthetic mass-spectrometry windows (signal vs. noise peaks) are available and you need ROC-based comparison of a classification method against intensity-threshold filtering to justify a denoising prefilter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-signal-classification-evaluation))
- **mass-spectrometry-signal-processing**: Use when a 1D MS signal (e.g., an extracted ion chromatogram or arrival time distribution from MZA data) needs peak detection, interpolation, or peak-area quantification, and detections must be validated against known peak positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-signal-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-software-cli-execution**: Use when you need to reproduce or benchmark compareMS2's pairwise tandem mass spectrum comparisons from the CLI — e.g. timing the 10 pairwise comparisons of five serum datasets under default parameters — rather than using the GUI. - **mass-spectrometry-spectral-alignment**: Use when you have a pair of MS/MS spectra—one from a known compound and one from a structurally modified variant of that compound—and you need to identify which atoms in the structure likely bear the modification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectral-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-spectral-embedding-learning**: Use when when you have preprocessed MS/MS spectral pairs (peak intensities and m/z values) and need to predict molecular structural similarity scores, or when you want to project spectra into a learned chemical embedding space for visualization (e.g., via UMAP) or downstream similarity searches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectral-embedding-learning) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-spectral-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw mass spectrometry spectra from an unknown analyte or a synthetic compound library and need to feed them into PS2MS or similar deep learning classifiers for NPS detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectral-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-archival-schema-design**: Use when a laboratory needs a central relational database schema to store, link, and archive mass spectra, peptide identifications, and quantitative results from MS-driven proteomics experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-archival-schema-design))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when when you have raw LC–QTOF wastewater spectra (or other real experimental mass spectrometry data) and need to feed them into a pretrained MSGO model for molecular structure prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when raw MS/MS peak files (e.g. mzML) must be converted into fixed-dimensional feature encodings/embeddings for downstream clustering or comparison of millions of spectra, following the GLEAMS learned-embedding approach. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing--c8552817647b5a01))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when raw MS/MS spectra (e.g. from an .mgf file like iPRG2012.mgf) must be cleaned and standardized before downstream analysis such as library searching or deep-neural-network processing, and peak lists need mz-range restriction, precursor/noise removal, and intensity scaling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing--99e25f8cc3a2c48f))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-pruning**: Decision support for cleaning a large, multi-source mass spectral library (e.g. .MSP, .MGF, .JSON, . - **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-quality-filtering**: Use when compiling or harmonizing MS/MS spectral libraries from multiple source repositories and you need to identify and remove spectra that fail quality thresholds (low resolution, precursor-fragment mass inconsistency, duplicate fragment patterns, noise-dominated, or missing critical metadata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-quality-filtering) · 2 copies)

- **mass-spectrometry-structure-elucidation-benchmarking**: Use when runtime benchmarking a GPU-accelerated DP structure-elucidation pipeline against a CPU DP baseline on natural-product testing datasets (e.g. Cuscuta chinensis, Ophiopogon japonicus, Polygonum multiflorum, Angelica sp.) at five decimal digits of mass. - **mass-spectrometry-substructure-assignment**: Use when after Mass2Motif discovery via LDA on preprocessed MS/MS spectra, when you have a set of inferred motifs (fragments and neutral losses with LDA probabilities) and need to assign putative substructure identities rather than retain anonymous motif labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-substructure-assignment) · 2 copies)

- **mass-spectrometry-table-export**: Use when you need per-scan base peak intensity and m/z across MS1-level scans of a Thermo raw file, e.g. to build a base-peak table for instrument QC or TIC-like diagnostics from rawR-readable Orbitrap data. - **mass-spectrometry-tolerance-calibration**: Use when after generating a feature table from mzML data (via Asari) and before performing MS1 or MS2 annotation. - **mass-spectrometry-tolerance-configuration**: Decision support for setting mass and RT matching tolerances when linking peak-picked features to in silico lipid precursor and fragment libraries in LipidMatch. - **mass-spectrometry-tolerance-window-calibration**: Use when after feature extraction from raw LC-MS or GC-MS data (using XCMS, MS-Dial, or similar), when you have a feature intensity table with m/z and RT metadata and a reference compound database (known molecules list with m/z, RT, and annotation metadata), and you need to assign confidence-ranked. - **mass-spectrometry-transient-signal-simulation**: Use when no experimentally acquired ground-truth high-resolution transients exist to benchmark reconstruction, and you need simulated MSI data with known peak centroids, adduct identities, and tissue-region assignments to quantify SNR gain and correlation fidelity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-transient-signal-simulation))
- **mass-spectrometry-transition-data-formatting**: Use when when you have raw mass spectrometry transition data from a triple-quadrupole or other tandem MS instrument and need to prepare it for suspect chemical screening using EISA-EXPOSOME, or when merging custom compound libraries into the T3DB format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-transition-data-formatting) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-transition-pair-counting**: Use when quantifying how fast a targeted-lipidomics tool enumerates precursor-product ion pairs for a transition list, or when reproducing LipidCreator's 60,000 pairs/s benchmark. - **mass-spectrometry-tune-data-loading**: Use when you have positive- or negative-mode tune reference compound data stored in HDF5 format (e.g., example_tune_pos.h5) and need to extract the tune mass spectrum for CCS calibration. - **mass-spectrometry-visualization-backend-comparison**: Use when you have a mass spectrometry visualization library that claims to support multiple plotting backends and need to verify that: (1) all backends produce functionally equivalent outputs, (2) execution times are consistent with reported benchmarks, and (3) the library scales appropriately. - **mass-spectrometry-wrapper-function-design**: Use when when you have a Spectra-based MS analysis workflow in R but need access to specialized spectral similarity, filtering, or normalization algorithms available only in Python libraries. - **mass-spectrum-adduct-assignment**: Use when when analyzing tandem mass spectra with unknown precursor adduct identity, especially for positive-mode data containing non-protonated adducts ([M+Na]+, [M+K]+, [M+NH4]+). - **mass-spectrum-averaging-and-processing**: Use when you have loaded a Bruker Solarix transient file (.d format with .ser or .fid content) and need to generate a processed mass spectrum for peak picking and molecular formula annotation. - **mass-spectrum-conditional-molecule-generation**: Use when you have a tandem mass spectrum plus an inferred molecular formula and need candidate molecular structures ranked by top-k accuracy, MCES distance, or Tanimoto similarity against a ground-truth molecule. - **mass-spectrum-database-matching**: Use when you have centroided LC-MS/MS spectral data (in MGF, mzXML, mzML, or mzData format) and want to identify known or predicted natural product structures present in your sample. - **mass-spectrum-de-novo-analysis**: Use when you have an unknown MS/MS spectrum (m/z and intensity pairs) from positive-mode ionization and need to identify the most likely molecular formula and adduct type (e.g., [M+H]+, [M+Na]+) when no reference library match is available or desirable. - **mass-spectrum-extraction-and-formatting**: Use when you have raw GC-MS data in netCDF or vendor-specific binary format and need to separate co-eluting compounds and extract clean mass spectra for each individual chemical component prior to molecular networking, spectral matching, or metabolite identification workflows. - **mass-spectrum-fragment-ion-extraction**: Use when you have an experimental MS/MS spectrum (e.g., from MassBank or acquired data) for a single metabolite with known accurate precursor m/z and adduct type, and you need to generate a library entry with scored fragments for use in metabolite annotation pipelines. - **mass-spectrum-matching-speed-profiling**: Use when you need to determine which component of a spectrum-matching pipeline (embedding representation vs. approximate nearest-neighbor index) drives recall@k accuracy versus query throughput, as verifiable by testing hybrid configurations on measured spectra. - **massive-task-orchestrator**: 极大体量任务协调框架。当面对需要重构大型代码库、同时修改多个关联文件、涉及跨系统同步更新（代码+配置+技能+自动化）等体量极大的复杂任务时使用。 触发词：体量极大、全面重构、多方向优化、跨文件联动修改、需要多Agent并行、大规模代码审计、逐项执行。 核心理念：拆解→规划→DAG→多Agent并行(波次)→独立验证→集成收口。 吸收来源：AugmentCode多Agent编排架构、agent-teams-playbook 6阶段工作流、Open-Multi-Agent DAG分解、LangGraph Supervisor/Handoff/Swarm模式。 **边界**：本技能负责**单会话内**的多 Agent 并行编排；**跨会话、多条指令批量下发、中途无反馈的长程任务**，其执行协议走 `long-task-executor`（本技能可被它调用，作为长程任务某一阶段的编排手段）。 Do NOT use for 单文件小改动、纯查询、一次就能做完的小任务（直接做，不必编排）。 **长报告的产出规范**（结构设计、编号重排、审计修复、待决策台账）走 `deep-research-report`； 本技能只负责**怎么把活儿并行下去**，不负责**产出物长什么样**。 ([2021feiyu/agent-work-skills](https://github.com/2021feiyu/agent-work-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/massive-task-orchestrator))

- **masst-repository-scale-search-workflow**: Use when you have a spectrum or feature of interest and want to know where else it occurs across all public metabolomics data — query preparation, repository-scale fastMASST search, specialized microbe/plant/food MASST for ecological context, and co-occurrence analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/workflows/masst-repository-scale-search) · 2 copies)
- **masst-repository-spectrum-searching**: Use when you hold a reference tandem MS/MS spectrum from a synthesized standard (e.g., bile amidate acquired in positive Q-ToF or negative Orbitrap mode) and need the set of public GNPS/MassIVE data files containing that molecule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/masst-repository-spectrum-searching))
